## Chest Overview

| Family | Focus | 3-Item Chest | Items | Guaranteed Focus Slots | Price (Robux) | 10-Item Chest | Items | Guaranteed Focus Slots | Price (Robux) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Blackwake | Weapons | Blackwake Cache | 3 | 1 | 399 | Blackwake Arsenal | 10 | 3 | 1049 |
| Ironhide | Armour (Body + Hats) | Ironhide Locker | 3 | 1 | 399 | Ironhide Bastion | 10 | 3 | 1049 |
| Relic | Artifacts | Relic Coffer | 3 | 1 | 399 | Relic Treasury | 10 | 3 | 1049 |
| Smuggler | Materials | Smuggler’s Stash | 3 | 1 | 349 | Smuggler’s Hoard | 10 | 3 | 999 |

| Uncommon | 27,20% |
| Rare | 21,10% |
| Epic | 12,00% |
| Legendary | 6,00% |
| Mythic | 3,00% |
| Treasure | 0,30% |
| Blessed | 0,05% |
| Cursed | 0,05% |

| Category | Blackwake<br>Guaranteed | Blackwake<br>Mixed | Ironhide<br>Guaranteed | Ironhide<br>Mixed | Relic<br>Guaranteed | Relic<br>Mixed | Smuggler<br>Guaranteed | Smuggler<br>Mixed |

| Body Armour | 0% | 12% | 50% | 30% | 0% | 10% | 0% | 10% |
| Hats | 0% | 12% | 50% | 30% | 0% | 10% | 0% | 10% |
| Artifacts | 0% | 11% | 0% | 15% | 100% | 55% | 0% | 10% |

### Rules &amp; Assumptions

| N.º | Regla |
| --- | --- |
| 1 | Each item slot first rolls a rarity, then a category (or guaranteed focus category), then an item inside that category + rarity. |
| 2 | Cheap chests contain 3 items: 1 guaranteed focus slot + 2 mixed slots. |
| 3 | Premium chests contain 10 items: 3 guaranteed focus slots + 7 mixed slots. |
| 4 | Rolls are independent and duplicate items are allowed. |
| 5 | Pets are excluded from all 8 Robux chests. |
| 6 | Artifact effects are intentionally omitted from this workbook and can be redesigned later. |
| 7 | Treasure, Blessed, and Cursed are separate rarity outcomes at 0.30%, 0.05%, and 0.05% respectively. |
| 8 | Full-chest item chance means probability of receiving at least one copy of that exact item. |
